    def _assert_pin(tool, reading):
        """Compare one reading against its recorded pin; a difference is an input error."""

    def qualified_run(project: ClientProject):
        """Two commits with a code delta; triage=run; record created; two notes."""
        project.write("src/app.py", "def f():\n    return 1\n")
        project.commit("base", ["src/app.py"])

E                           --side-by-side | --files | --stat | --focus <path> | --since <ref> | --head <ref>
E                           --worktree/-w: 审查未提交改动 (HEAD vs 工作区, 含已暂存+未暂存)
E                 review    交互式逐文件 Review (测试文件优先; --yes 非交互; --no-tests-first 关闭排序)
E                 note      非交互追加一条 Review 意见 (供 Agent/CI 调用, 无需 delta)
E                           -s <severity> -f <file> [-l <line>] -c <comment> [--review-file <path>]
E                           severity: blocking | important | suggestion | nitpick | question | praise
E                 report    查看/汇总 Review 报告
E                           --list (默认) | --latest | --summary | --cat <file> | --file <record>
E                           --summary --file <code-review.md>: 合入门禁判决的唯一来源
E                           退出码关闭于 0/1/2: 0 允许, 1 拒绝 (当前记录的 blocking 计数), 2 输入错误
E                           省略 --file 是历史模式: 自标 implicit, 组合路径一律显式给路径
E                 base      解析评审基准 (非交互路径第 1 步)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since/--base <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --head <ref> | --json
E                           输出 base= head= base_source=; 无档可解时 base_source=not-evaluated 并退出 2
E                 triage    判定代码段适用性 (run | not-applicable | not-evaluated)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --code-paths <glob[,glob]>
E                           输出 triage= basis= changed_files=; 空 delta 在任何回执之前判定
E                 start     创建代码段记录, 并把上一次记录轮转归档 (非交互路径第 2 步)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --renderer <delta|text>
E                           --triage <value>: 非 run 的适用性拒绝创建记录; 归档名 code-review-<YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ>.md
E                 receipt   覆盖率回执与段裁决 (非交互路径第 4 步)
E                           --review-file <code-review.md> (必需, 不做目录内最新文件隐式选取)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --renderer <delta|text>
E                           --skip-with-reason <glob> --reason <reason>  (可重复; 理由取自法定五值)
E                           写出记录内定界的 name=value 块并打印同一块; 四项计数只由它产出
E                           退出码关闭于 0/1/2: 0 完整, 1 覆盖缺口, 2 不可评估 (不写块)
E             
E               NON-INTERACTIVE PATH (four steps, in this order — four, never three):
E                 1. base      resolve the range: base= head= base_source=
E                 2. start     record creation and rotation (required from the second run on)
E                 3. note      append opinions (repeatable, one call per opinion)
E                 4. receipt   coverage receipt and the summary verdict
E               No step prompts; no step needs a terminal. Step 2 is a step of its own: skipping
E               it turns the unattended path into an agent hand-writing the record.
E             
E               EXAMPLES:
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --stat --since main          # Phase 1: 提交粒度自检
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --worktree                   # 审查未提交改动 (dogfooding/提交前)
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --side-by-side               # 左右分栏对比 (GitHub PR 视图)
E                 git-delta-review.sh review --since main               # Phase 2: 结构化审查
E                 git-delta-review.sh note -s blocking -f src/a.rs -l 45 -c "空指针风险"
E                 git-delta-review.sh report --summary                  # Phase 3/4: 收敛判定
E                 git-delta-review.sh base --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 解析本次评审区间
E                 git-delta-review.sh triage --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 适用性判定
E                 git-delta-review.sh start --spec .specify/specs/<key>   # 代码段: 创建/轮转记录
E             
E               ENVIRONMENT:
E                 REVIEW_DIR    Review 文件存储目录 (默认: ${SKILL_WORKDIR}/.specify/review)
E                 GIT_BASE      默认对比基准 (默认: main; base/triage/start 不使用该默认)
E                 GIT_HEAD      默认对比目标 (默认: HEAD)
E                 DELTA_BIN     delta 二进制路径 (默认: delta)
E                 REVIEW_RUN_TIMESTAMP  固定运行时间戳 YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ (默认: 当前 UTC 时间)
E             side-effects: writes only through the options it documents; a flag-only invocation writes nothing
E             exit-codes: 0 success; 1 a documented check failed; 2 argument or environment error

E                 2. start     record creation and rotation (required from the second run on)
E                 3. note      append opinions (repeatable, one call per opinion)
E                 4. receipt   coverage receipt and the summary verdict
E               No step prompts; no step needs a terminal. Step 2 is a step of its own: skipping
E               it turns the unattended path into an agent hand-writing the record.
E             
E               EXAMPLES:
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --stat --since main          # Phase 1: 提交粒度自检
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --worktree                   # 审查未提交改动 (dogfooding/提交前)
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --side-by-side               # 左右分栏对比 (GitHub PR 视图)
E                 git-delta-review.sh review --since main               # Phase 2: 结构化审查
E                 git-delta-review.sh note -s blocking -f src/a.rs -l 45 -c "空指针风险"
E                 git-delta-review.sh report --summary                  # Phase 3/4: 收敛判定
E                 git-delta-review.sh base --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 解析本次评审区间
E                 git-delta-review.sh triage --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 适用性判定
E                 git-delta-review.sh start --spec .specify/specs/<key>   # 代码段: 创建/轮转记录
E             
E               ENVIRONMENT:
E                 REVIEW_DIR    Review 文件存储目录 (默认: ${SKILL_WORKDIR}/.specify/review)
E                 GIT_BASE      默认对比基准 (默认: main; base/triage/start 不使用该默认)
E                 GIT_HEAD      默认对比目标 (默认: HEAD)
E                 DELTA_BIN     delta 二进制路径 (默认: delta)
E                 REVIEW_RUN_TIMESTAMP  固定运行时间戳 YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ (默认: 当前 UTC 时间)

    def qualified_run(project: ClientProject):
        """Two commits with a code delta; triage=run; record created; two notes."""
        project.write("src/app.py", "def f():\n    return 1\n")
        project.commit("base", ["src/app.py"])

        assert triage.returncode == 0 and "triage=run" in triage.stdout, triage.describe()
        start = run_review(project, "start", "--spec", SPEC_DIR,
                           "--since", "HEAD~1", "--head", "HEAD", "--triage", "run")
        assert start.returncode == 0, start.describe()
        record = project.path / RECORD_REL
        assert record.is_file()
        for sev, comment in (("blocking", "defect body alpha"), ("praise", "nice work beta")):
            note = run_review(project, "note", "-s", sev, "-f", "src/app.py",
                              "-c", comment, "--review-file", RECORD_REL)

E             stdout:

E             stderr:
E             error: unrecognized option: -f
E             
E               git-delta-review — git-delta 驱动的命令行 Code Review 工作流
E               (看 → 批 → 记 → 报 四步闭环; 方法论见 references/review-workflow.md)
E             
E               USAGE:
E                 git-delta-review.sh <command> [options]
E             
E               COMMANDS:
E                 diff      渲染人类友好的代码对比 (git-delta 核心能力)
E                           --side-by-side | --files | --stat | --focus <path> | --since <ref> | --head <ref>
E                           --worktree/-w: 审查未提交改动 (HEAD vs 工作区, 含已暂存+未暂存)
E                 review    交互式逐文件 Review (测试文件优先; --yes 非交互; --no-tests-first 关闭排序)
E                 note      非交互追加一条 Review 意见 (供 Agent/CI 调用, 无需 delta)
E                           -s <severity> -f <file> [-l <line>] -c <comment> [--review-file <path>]
E                           severity: blocking | important | suggestion | nitpick | question | praise
E                 report    查看/汇总 Review 报告
E                           --list (默认) | --latest | --summary | --cat <file> | --file <record>
E                           --summary --file <code-review.md>: 合入门禁判决的唯一来源
E                           退出码关闭于 0/1/2: 0 允许, 1 拒绝 (当前记录的 blocking 计数), 2 输入错误
E                           省略 --file 是历史模式: 自标 implicit, 组合路径一律显式给路径
E                 base      解析评审基准 (非交互路径第 1 步)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since/--base <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --head <ref> | --json
E                           输出 base= head= base_source=; 无档可解时 base_source=not-evaluated 并退出 2
E                 triage    判定代码段适用性 (run | not-applicable | not-evaluated)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --code-paths <glob[,glob]>
E                           输出 triage= basis= changed_files=; 空 delta 在任何回执之前判定
E                 start     创建代码段记录, 并把上一次记录轮转归档 (非交互路径第 2 步)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --renderer <delta|text>
E                           --triage <value>: 非 run 的适用性拒绝创建记录; 归档名 code-review-<YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ>.md
E                 receipt   覆盖率回执与段裁决 (非交互路径第 4 步)
E                           --review-file <code-review.md> (必需, 不做目录内最新文件隐式选取)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --renderer <delta|text>
E                           --skip-with-reason <glob> --reason <reason>  (可重复; 理由取自法定五值)
E                           写出记录内定界的 name=value 块并打印同一块; 四项计数只由它产出
E                           退出码关闭于 0/1/2: 0 完整, 1 覆盖缺口, 2 不可评估 (不写块)
E             
E               NON-INTERACTIVE PATH (four steps, in this order — four, never three):
E                 1. base      resolve the range: base= head= base_source=
E                 2. start     record creation and rotation (required from the second run on)
E                 3. note      append opinions (repeatable, one call per opinion)
E                 4. receipt   coverage receipt and the summary verdict
E               No step prompts; no step needs a terminal. Step 2 is a step of its own: skipping
E               it turns the unattended path into an agent hand-writing the record.
E             
E               EXAMPLES:
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --stat --since main          # Phase 1: 提交粒度自检
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --worktree                   # 审查未提交改动 (dogfooding/提交前)
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --side-by-side               # 左右分栏对比 (GitHub PR 视图)
E                 git-delta-review.sh review --since main               # Phase 2: 结构化审查
E                 git-delta-review.sh note -s blocking -f src/a.rs -l 45 -c "空指针风险"
E                 git-delta-review.sh report --summary                  # Phase 3/4: 收敛判定
E                 git-delta-review.sh base --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 解析本次评审区间
E                 git-delta-review.sh triage --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 适用性判定
E                 git-delta-review.sh start --spec .specify/specs/<key>   # 代码段: 创建/轮转记录
E             
E               ENVIRONMENT:
E                 REVIEW_DIR    Review 文件存储目录 (默认: ${SKILL_WORKDIR}/.specify/review)
E                 GIT_BASE      默认对比基准 (默认: main; base/triage/start 不使用该默认)

    def qualified_run(project: ClientProject):
        """Two commits with a code delta; triage=run; record created; two notes."""
        project.write("src/app.py", "def f():\n    return 1\n")
        project.commit("base", ["src/app.py"])
        project.write("src/app.py", "def f():\n    return 2\n")
        project.commit("change", ["src/app.py"])
        triage = run_review(project, "triage", "--since", "HEAD~1", "--head", "HEAD")
        assert triage.returncode == 0 and "triage=run" in triage.stdout, triage.describe()
        start = run_review(project, "start", "--spec", SPEC_DIR,
                           "--since", "HEAD~1", "--head", "HEAD", "--triage", "run")
        assert start.returncode == 0, start.describe()
        record = project.path / RECORD_REL
        assert record.is_file()
        for sev, comment in (("blocking", "defect body alpha"), ("praise", "nice work beta")):
            note = run_review(project, "note", "-s", sev, "-f", "src/app.py",
                              "-c", comment, "--review-file", RECORD_REL)
>           assert note.returncode == 0, note.describe()
E           AssertionError: rc=2
E             cmd=bash /cws_data/cloud-native-ai/spec-kit/skills/internal/code-review/scripts/git-delta-review.sh note -s blocking -f src/app.py -c defect body alpha --review-file .specify/specs/072-demo/code-review.md
E             stdout:
E             
E             stderr:
E             error: unrecognized option: -f
E             
E               git-delta-review — git-delta 驱动的命令行 Code Review 工作流
E               (看 → 批 → 记 → 报 四步闭环; 方法论见 references/review-workflow.md)
E             
E               USAGE:
E                 git-delta-review.sh <command> [options]
E             
E               COMMANDS:
E                 diff      渲染人类友好的代码对比 (git-delta 核心能力)
E                           --side-by-side | --files | --stat | --focus <path> | --since <ref> | --head <ref>
E                           --worktree/-w: 审查未提交改动 (HEAD vs 工作区, 含已暂存+未暂存)
E                 review    交互式逐文件 Review (测试文件优先; --yes 非交互; --no-tests-first 关闭排序)
E                 note      非交互追加一条 Review 意见 (供 Agent/CI 调用, 无需 delta)
E                           -s <severity> -f <file> [-l <line>] -c <comment> [--review-file <path>]
E                           severity: blocking | important | suggestion | nitpick | question | praise
E                 report    查看/汇总 Review 报告
E                           --list (默认) | --latest | --summary | --cat <file> | --file <record>
E                           --summary --file <code-review.md>: 合入门禁判决的唯一来源
E                           退出码关闭于 0/1/2: 0 允许, 1 拒绝 (当前记录的 blocking 计数), 2 输入错误
E                           省略 --file 是历史模式: 自标 implicit, 组合路径一律显式给路径
E                 base      解析评审基准 (非交互路径第 1 步)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since/--base <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --head <ref> | --json
E                           输出 base= head= base_source=; 无档可解时 base_source=not-evaluated 并退出 2
E                 triage    判定代码段适用性 (run | not-applicable | not-evaluated)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --code-paths <glob[,glob]>
E                           输出 triage= basis= changed_files=; 空 delta 在任何回执之前判定
E                 start     创建代码段记录, 并把上一次记录轮转归档 (非交互路径第 2 步)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --renderer <delta|text>
E                           --triage <value>: 非 run 的适用性拒绝创建记录; 归档名 code-review-<YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ>.md
E                 receipt   覆盖率回执与段裁决 (非交互路径第 4 步)
E                           --review-file <code-review.md> (必需, 不做目录内最新文件隐式选取)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --renderer <delta|text>
E                           --skip-with-reason <glob> --reason <reason>  (可重复; 理由取自法定五值)
E                           写出记录内定界的 name=value 块并打印同一块; 四项计数只由它产出
E                           退出码关闭于 0/1/2: 0 完整, 1 覆盖缺口, 2 不可评估 (不写块)
E             
E               NON-INTERACTIVE PATH (four steps, in this order — four, never three):
E                 1. base      resolve the range: base= head= base_source=
E                 2. start     record creation and rotation (required from the second run on)
E                 3. note      append opinions (repeatable, one call per opinion)
E                 4. receipt   coverage receipt and the summary verdict
E               No step prompts; no step needs a terminal. Step 2 is a step of its own: skipping
E               it turns the unattended path into an agent hand-writing the record.
E             
E               EXAMPLES:
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --stat --since main          # Phase 1: 提交粒度自检
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --worktree                   # 审查未提交改动 (dogfooding/提交前)
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --side-by-side               # 左右分栏对比 (GitHub PR 视图)
E                 git-delta-review.sh review --since main               # Phase 2: 结构化审查
E                 git-delta-review.sh note -s blocking -f src/a.rs -l 45 -c "空指针风险"
E                 git-delta-review.sh report --summary                  # Phase 3/4: 收敛判定
E                 git-delta-review.sh base --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 解析本次评审区间
E                 git-delta-review.sh triage --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 适用性判定
E                 git-delta-review.sh start --spec .specify/specs/<key>   # 代码段: 创建/轮转记录
E             
E               ENVIRONMENT:
E                 REVIEW_DIR    Review 文件存储目录 (默认: ${SKILL_WORKDIR}/.specify/review)
E                 GIT_BASE      默认对比基准 (默认: main; base/triage/start 不使用该默认)
E                 GIT_HEAD      默认对比目标 (默认: HEAD)
E                 DELTA_BIN     delta 二进制路径 (默认: delta)
E                 REVIEW_RUN_TIMESTAMP  固定运行时间戳 YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ (默认: 当前 UTC 时间)
E             side-effects: writes only through the options it documents; a flag-only invocation writes nothing
E             exit-codes: 0 success; 1 a documented check failed; 2 argument or environment error
E             
E           assert 2 == 0
E            +  where 2 = EngineRun(argv=['bash', '/cws_data/cloud-native-ai/spec-kit/skills/internal/code-review/scripts/git-delta-review.sh', ...only invocation writes nothing\nexit-codes: 0 success; 1 a documented check failed; 2 argument or environment error\n').returncode

project = <fixtures.client_project.ClientProject object at 0x7f69bdff6810>

    def qualified_run(project: ClientProject):
        """Two commits with a code delta; triage=run; record created; two notes."""
        project.write("src/app.py", "def f():\n    return 1\n")
        project.commit("base", ["src/app.py"])
        project.write("src/app.py", "def f():\n    return 2\n")
        project.commit("change", ["src/app.py"])
        triage = run_review(project, "triage", "--since", "HEAD~1", "--head", "HEAD")
        assert triage.returncode == 0 and "triage=run" in triage.stdout, triage.describe()
        start = run_review(project, "start", "--spec", SPEC_DIR,
                           "--since", "HEAD~1", "--head", "HEAD", "--triage", "run")
        assert start.returncode == 0, start.describe()
        record = project.path / RECORD_REL
        assert record.is_file()
        for sev, comment in (("blocking", "defect body alpha"), ("praise", "nice work beta")):
            note = run_review(project, "note", "-s", sev, "-f", "src/app.py",
                              "-c", comment, "--review-file", RECORD_REL)
>           assert note.returncode == 0, note.describe()
E           AssertionError: rc=2
E             cmd=bash /cws_data/cloud-native-ai/spec-kit/skills/internal/code-review/scripts/git-delta-review.sh note -s blocking -f src/app.py -c defect body alpha --review-file .specify/specs/072-demo/code-review.md
E             stdout:
E             
E             stderr:
E             error: unrecognized option: -f
E             
E               git-delta-review — git-delta 驱动的命令行 Code Review 工作流
E               (看 → 批 → 记 → 报 四步闭环; 方法论见 references/review-workflow.md)
E             
E               USAGE:
E                 git-delta-review.sh <command> [options]
E             
E               COMMANDS:
E                 diff      渲染人类友好的代码对比 (git-delta 核心能力)
E                           --side-by-side | --files | --stat | --focus <path> | --since <ref> | --head <ref>
E                           --worktree/-w: 审查未提交改动 (HEAD vs 工作区, 含已暂存+未暂存)
E                 review    交互式逐文件 Review (测试文件优先; --yes 非交互; --no-tests-first 关闭排序)
E                 note      非交互追加一条 Review 意见 (供 Agent/CI 调用, 无需 delta)
E                           -s <severity> -f <file> [-l <line>] -c <comment> [--review-file <path>]
E                           severity: blocking | important | suggestion | nitpick | question | praise
E                 report    查看/汇总 Review 报告
E                           --list (默认) | --latest | --summary | --cat <file> | --file <record>
E                           --summary --file <code-review.md>: 合入门禁判决的唯一来源
E                           退出码关闭于 0/1/2: 0 允许, 1 拒绝 (当前记录的 blocking 计数), 2 输入错误
E                           省略 --file 是历史模式: 自标 implicit, 组合路径一律显式给路径
E                 base      解析评审基准 (非交互路径第 1 步)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since/--base <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --head <ref> | --json
E                           输出 base= head= base_source=; 无档可解时 base_source=not-evaluated 并退出 2
E                 triage    判定代码段适用性 (run | not-applicable | not-evaluated)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --code-paths <glob[,glob]>
E                           输出 triage= basis= changed_files=; 空 delta 在任何回执之前判定
E                 start     创建代码段记录, 并把上一次记录轮转归档 (非交互路径第 2 步)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --renderer <delta|text>
E                           --triage <value>: 非 run 的适用性拒绝创建记录; 归档名 code-review-<YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ>.md
E                 receipt   覆盖率回执与段裁决 (非交互路径第 4 步)
E                           --review-file <code-review.md> (必需, 不做目录内最新文件隐式选取)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --renderer <delta|text>
E                           --skip-with-reason <glob> --reason <reason>  (可重复; 理由取自法定五值)
E                           写出记录内定界的 name=value 块并打印同一块; 四项计数只由它产出
E                           退出码关闭于 0/1/2: 0 完整, 1 覆盖缺口, 2 不可评估 (不写块)
E             
E               NON-INTERACTIVE PATH (four steps, in this order — four, never three):
E                 1. base      resolve the range: base= head= base_source=
E                 2. start     record creation and rotation (required from the second run on)
E                 3. note      append opinions (repeatable, one call per opinion)
E                 4. receipt   coverage receipt and the summary verdict
E               No step prompts; no step needs a terminal. Step 2 is a step of its own: skipping
E               it turns the unattended path into an agent hand-writing the record.
E             
E               EXAMPLES:
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --stat --since main          # Phase 1: 提交粒度自检
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --worktree                   # 审查未提交改动 (dogfooding/提交前)
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --side-by-side               # 左右分栏对比 (GitHub PR 视图)
E                 git-delta-review.sh review --since main               # Phase 2: 结构化审查
E                 git-delta-review.sh note -s blocking -f src/a.rs -l 45 -c "空指针风险"
E                 git-delta-review.sh report --summary                  # Phase 3/4: 收敛判定
E                 git-delta-review.sh base --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 解析本次评审区间
E                 git-delta-review.sh triage --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 适用性判定
E                 git-delta-review.sh start --spec .specify/specs/<key>   # 代码段: 创建/轮转记录
E             
E               ENVIRONMENT:
E                 REVIEW_DIR    Review 文件存储目录 (默认: ${SKILL_WORKDIR}/.specify/review)
E                 GIT_BASE      默认对比基准 (默认: main; base/triage/start 不使用该默认)
E                 GIT_HEAD      默认对比目标 (默认: HEAD)
E                 DELTA_BIN     delta 二进制路径 (默认: delta)
E                 REVIEW_RUN_TIMESTAMP  固定运行时间戳 YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ (默认: 当前 UTC 时间)
E             side-effects: writes only through the options it documents; a flag-only invocation writes nothing
E             exit-codes: 0 success; 1 a documented check failed; 2 argument or environment error
E             
E           assert 2 == 0
E            +  where 2 = EngineRun(argv=['bash', '/cws_data/cloud-native-ai/spec-kit/skills/internal/code-review/scripts/git-delta-review.sh', ...only invocation writes nothing\nexit-codes: 0 success; 1 a documented check failed; 2 argument or environment error\n').returncode

E                 review    交互式逐文件 Review (测试文件优先; --yes 非交互; --no-tests-first 关闭排序)
E                 note      非交互追加一条 Review 意见 (供 Agent/CI 调用, 无需 delta)

E                           severity: blocking | important | suggestion | nitpick | question | praise
E                 report    查看/汇总 Review 报告
E                           --list (默认) | --latest | --summary | --cat <file> | --file <record>
E                           --summary --file <code-review.md>: 合入门禁判决的唯一来源
E                           退出码关闭于 0/1/2: 0 允许, 1 拒绝 (当前记录的 blocking 计数), 2 输入错误
E                           省略 --file 是历史模式: 自标 implicit, 组合路径一律显式给路径
E                 base      解析评审基准 (非交互路径第 1 步)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since/--base <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --head <ref> | --json
E                           输出 base= head= base_source=; 无档可解时 base_source=not-evaluated 并退出 2
E                 triage    判定代码段适用性 (run | not-applicable | not-evaluated)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --code-paths <glob[,glob]>
E                           输出 triage= basis= changed_files=; 空 delta 在任何回执之前判定
E                 start     创建代码段记录, 并把上一次记录轮转归档 (非交互路径第 2 步)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --renderer <delta|text>
E                           --triage <value>: 非 run 的适用性拒绝创建记录; 归档名 code-review-<YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ>.md
E                 receipt   覆盖率回执与段裁决 (非交互路径第 4 步)
E                           --review-file <code-review.md> (必需, 不做目录内最新文件隐式选取)
E                           --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --renderer <delta|text>
E                           --skip-with-reason <glob> --reason <reason>  (可重复; 理由取自法定五值)
E                           写出记录内定界的 name=value 块并打印同一块; 四项计数只由它产出
E                           退出码关闭于 0/1/2: 0 完整, 1 覆盖缺口, 2 不可评估 (不写块)
E             
E               NON-INTERACTIVE PATH (four steps, in this order — four, never three):
E                 1. base      resolve the range: base= head= base_source=
E                 2. start     record creation and rotation (required from the second run on)
E                 3. note      append opinions (repeatable, one call per opinion)
E                 4. receipt   coverage receipt and the summary verdict
E               No step prompts; no step needs a terminal. Step 2 is a step of its own: skipping
E               it turns the unattended path into an agent hand-writing the record.
E             
E               EXAMPLES:
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --stat --since main          # Phase 1: 提交粒度自检
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --worktree                   # 审查未提交改动 (dogfooding/提交前)
E                 git-delta-review.sh diff --side-by-side               # 左右分栏对比 (GitHub PR 视图)
E                 git-delta-review.sh review --since main               # Phase 2: 结构化审查
E                 git-delta-review.sh note -s blocking -f src/a.rs -l 45 -c "空指针风险"
E                 git-delta-review.sh report --summary                  # Phase 3/4: 收敛判定
E                 git-delta-review.sh base --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 解析本次评审区间
E                 git-delta-review.sh triage --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 适用性判定
E                 git-delta-review.sh start --spec .specify/specs/<key>   # 代码段: 创建/轮转记录

E         
E           git-delta-review — git-delta 驱动的命令行 Code Review 工作流
E           (看 → 批 → 记 → 报 四步闭环; 方法论见 references/review-workflow.md)
E         
E           USAGE:
E             git-delta-review.sh <command> [options]
E         
E           COMMANDS:
E             diff      渲染人类友好的代码对比 (git-delta 核心能力)
E                       --side-by-side | --files | --stat | --focus <path> | --since <ref> | --head <ref>
E                       --worktree/-w: 审查未提交改动 (HEAD vs 工作区, 含已暂存+未暂存)
E             review    交互式逐文件 Review (测试文件优先; --yes 非交互; --no-tests-first 关闭排序)
E             note      非交互追加一条 Review 意见 (供 Agent/CI 调用, 无需 delta)
E                       -s <severity> -f <file> [-l <line>] -c <comment> [--review-file <path>]
E                       severity: blocking | important | suggestion | nitpick | question | praise
E             report    查看/汇总 Review 报告
E                       --list (默认) | --latest | --summary | --cat <file> | --file <record>
E                       --summary --file <code-review.md>: 合入门禁判决的唯一来源
E                       退出码关闭于 0/1/2: 0 允许, 1 拒绝 (当前记录的 blocking 计数), 2 输入错误
E                       省略 --file 是历史模式: 自标 implicit, 组合路径一律显式给路径
E             base      解析评审基准 (非交互路径第 1 步)
E                       --spec <dir> | --since/--base <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --head <ref> | --json
E                       输出 base= head= base_source=; 无档可解时 base_source=not-evaluated 并退出 2
E             triage    判定代码段适用性 (run | not-applicable | not-evaluated)
E                       --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --code-paths <glob[,glob]>
E                       输出 triage= basis= changed_files=; 空 delta 在任何回执之前判定
E             start     创建代码段记录, 并把上一次记录轮转归档 (非交互路径第 2 步)
E                       --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --renderer <delta|text>
E                       --triage <value>: 非 run 的适用性拒绝创建记录; 归档名 code-review-<YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ>.md
E             receipt   覆盖率回执与段裁决 (非交互路径第 4 步)
E                       --review-file <code-review.md> (必需, 不做目录内最新文件隐式选取)
E                       --spec <dir> | --since <ref> | --head <ref> | --trunk <ref> | --renderer <delta|text>
E                       --skip-with-reason <glob> --reason <reason>  (可重复; 理由取自法定五值)
E                       写出记录内定界的 name=value 块并打印同一块; 四项计数只由它产出
E                       退出码关闭于 0/1/2: 0 完整, 1 覆盖缺口, 2 不可评估 (不写块)
E         
E           NON-INTERACTIVE PATH (four steps, in this order — four, never three):
E             1. base      resolve the range: base= head= base_source=
E             2. start     record creation and rotation (required from the second run on)
E             3. note      append opinions (repeatable, one call per opinion)
E             4. receipt   coverage receipt and the summary verdict
E           No step prompts; no step needs a terminal. Step 2 is a step of its own: skipping
E           it turns the unattended path into an agent hand-writing the record.
E         
E           EXAMPLES:
E             git-delta-review.sh diff --stat --since main          # Phase 1: 提交粒度自检
E             git-delta-review.sh diff --worktree                   # 审查未提交改动 (dogfooding/提交前)
E             git-delta-review.sh diff --side-by-side               # 左右分栏对比 (GitHub PR 视图)
E             git-delta-review.sh review --since main               # Phase 2: 结构化审查
E             git-delta-review.sh note -s blocking -f src/a.rs -l 45 -c "空指针风险"
E             git-delta-review.sh report --summary                  # Phase 3/4: 收敛判定
E             git-delta-review.sh base --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 解析本次评审区间
E             git-delta-review.sh triage --spec .specify/specs/<key>  # 代码段: 适用性判定
E             git-delta-review.sh start --spec .specify/specs/<key>   # 代码段: 创建/轮转记录
E         
E           ENVIRONMENT:
E             REVIEW_DIR    Review 文件存储目录 (默认: ${SKILL_WORKDIR}/.specify/review)
E             GIT_BASE      默认对比基准 (默认: main; base/triage/start 不使用该默认)
E             GIT_HEAD      默认对比目标 (默认: HEAD)
E             DELTA_BIN     delta 二进制路径 (默认: delta)
E             REVIEW_RUN_TIMESTAMP  固定运行时间戳 YYYYMMDDTHHMMSSZ (默认: 当前 UTC 时间)
E         side-effects: writes only through the options it documents; a flag-only invocation writes nothing
E         exit-codes: 0 success; 1 a documented check failed; 2 argument or environment error
E         
E       assert 2 == 0
E        +  where 2 = ScriptRun(argv=['bash', '/cws_data/cloud-native-ai/spec-kit/skills/internal/code-review/scripts/git-delta-review.sh', ...only invocation writes nothing\nexit-codes: 0 success; 1 a documented check failed; 2 argument or environment error\n').returncode

    def test_t018_the_interactive_walk_and_the_six_level_validation_are_intact(
        repo: ReviewGitRepo,
    ):
        """Driven end to end, not read off the script (C-28)."""
        delta = ReviewGitRepo.host_delta_bin()
        if not delta:
            pytest.skip("no renderer on this host; the interactive walk needs one")
    
        shapes = _standard(repo)
        walk = repo.run_script(
            "review",
            "--yes",
            "--since",
            shapes["code"].base,
            "--head",
            shapes["code"].head,
            delta_bin=delta,
        )
        assert walk.returncode == 0, (
            "the non-interactive per-file walk MUST still complete:\n" + walk.describe()
        )
        produced = sorted(repo.review_dir.glob("*.md"))
        assert len(produced) == 1, f"the walk MUST still write its report, found {produced}"
        body = produced[0].read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert repo.CODE_PATH in body and "统计" in body, (
            "the walk MUST still render every changed file and its statistics table"
        )
    
        severities = ("blocking", "important", "suggestion", "nitpick", "question", "praise")
        for severity in severities:
            noted = repo.run_script(
                "note",
                "-s",
                severity,
                "-f",
                repo.CODE_PATH,
                "-c",
                f"{severity} level accepted",
                "--review-file",
                str(produced[0]),
            )

popenargs = (['/cws_data/cloud-native-ai/spec-kit/.specify/scripts/bash/create-new-tools.sh', '--json', '--name', 'definitely-not-a-real-tool-7ac4f7b4', '--action', 'find'],)

stderr = '  File "<stdin>", line 7\n    json.loads(text)\n    ^\nIndentationError: expected an indented block after \'try\' statement on line 6\n'
retcode = 1

    def run(*popenargs,
            input=None, capture_output=False, timeout=None, check=False, **kwargs):
        """Run command with arguments and return a CompletedProcess instance.
    
        The returned instance will have attributes args, returncode, stdout and
        stderr. By default, stdout and stderr are not captured, and those attributes
        will be None. Pass stdout=PIPE and/or stderr=PIPE in order to capture them.
    
        If check is True and the exit code was non-zero, it raises a
        CalledProcessError. The CalledProcessError object will have the return code
        in the returncode attribute, and output & stderr attributes if those streams
        were captured.
    
        If timeout is given, and the process takes too long, a TimeoutExpired
        exception will be raised.
    
        There is an optional argument "input", allowing you to
        pass bytes or a string to the subprocess's stdin.  If you use this argument
        you may not also use the Popen constructor's "stdin" argument, as
        it will be used internally.
    
        By default, all communication is in bytes, and therefore any "input" should
        be bytes, and the stdout and stderr will be bytes. If in text mode, any
        "input" should be a string, and stdout and stderr will be strings decoded
        according to locale encoding, or by "encoding" if set. Text mode is
        triggered by setting any of text, encoding, errors or universal_newlines.
    
        The other arguments are the same as for the Popen constructor.
        """
        if input is not None:
            if kwargs.get('stdin') is not None:
                raise ValueError('stdin and input arguments may not both be used.')
            kwargs['stdin'] = PIPE
    
        if capture_output:
            if kwargs.get('stdout') is not None or kwargs.get('stderr') is not None:
                raise ValueError('stdout and stderr arguments may not be used '
                                 'with capture_output.')
            kwargs['stdout'] = PIPE
            kwargs['stderr'] = PIPE
    
        with Popen(*popenargs, **kwargs) as process:
            try:
                stdout, stderr = process.communicate(input, timeout=timeout)
            except TimeoutExpired as exc:
                process.kill()
                if _mswindows:
                    # Windows accumulates the output in a single blocking
                    # read() call run on child threads, with the timeout
                    # being done in a join() on those threads.  communicate()
                    # _after_ kill() is required to collect that and add it
                    # to the exception.
                    exc.stdout, exc.stderr = process.communicate()
                else:
                    # POSIX _communicate already populated the output so
                    # far into the TimeoutExpired exception.
                    process.wait()
                raise
            except:  # Including KeyboardInterrupt, communicate handled that.
                process.kill()
                # We don't call process.wait() as .__exit__ does that for us.
                raise
            retcode = process.poll()
            if check and retcode:
>               raise CalledProcessError(retcode, process.args,
                                         output=stdout, stderr=stderr)
E               subprocess.CalledProcessError: Command '['/cws_data/cloud-native-ai/spec-kit/.specify/scripts/bash/create-new-tools.sh', '--json', '--name', 'definitely-not-a-real-tool-7ac4f7b4', '--action', 'find']' returned non-zero exit status 1.

text = None

    def preparse_version_guard(argv: Optional[Iterable[str]] = None,
                               script_name: Optional[str] = None,
                               stream=None,
                               declared: Optional[Iterable[str]] = None,
                               text: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        """Answer `--version` (and reject an unknown option) BEFORE any parser reads `argv`.
    
        Two situations need this: a script that reads `sys.argv` by hand (no argparse at all),
        and a script that builds its parser late. Calling it at the top of `main()` gives the
        same observable surface as `VersionAction` without requiring the parser to exist yet.
    
        Scanning stops at the bare `--` separator, so an argument after it is never treated as
        an unknown option (C-9). A token that is a known option never rescues an unknown one:
        every `-*` token before the separator is judged on its own.
    
        `--help` / `-h` are deliberately NOT handled here — a hand-rolled parser usually has
        its own help path, and swallowing it would change an existing interface (FR-018). They
        are also not REJECTED: a guard that refused `--help` would break the very interface it
        exists to protect, so they are skipped and left to the module's own parser.
    
        `declared` is the script's own option vocabulary — every option its parser accepts. It
        MUST be supplied by a script that HAS options, or the guard would refuse a legal one
        (measured on `sync-mirrors.py --check` while landing T060). A script with no options
        passes nothing, and then every `-*` token is undeclared, which is the truthful answer.
    
        The scan judges the WHOLE line before answering anything, which is C-9's sharp leg:
        `--help --bogus` and `--version --bogus` are both red, because a recognized token in
        the same line never rescues an unknown one. Answering while scanning left-to-right would
        let `--version --bogus` pass, so the line is judged first and answered second.
    
        `text`, when given, is the module's own source: see `guard_argv`.
        """
        source = text
        args: List[str] = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
        name = script_name or (sys.argv[0] if sys.argv and sys.argv[0] else "script")
        err = sys.stderr if stream is None else stream
        known = set(declared or ())
        unjudged = args[:args.index(SEPARATOR)] if SEPARATOR in args else args
        for token in unjudged:
            if token in ("-h", "--help", VERSION_FLAG):
                continue                       # the module's own parser answers help
            if token.startswith("-") and token != "-" and token not in known:
                print("error: unrecognized option: %s" % token, file=err)
>               raise SystemExit(UNKNOWN_OPTION_EXIT)
E               SystemExit: 2

___________ test_c17_real_tree_live_size_is_within_the_raised_budget ___________

    @pytest.mark.contract
    def test_c17_real_tree_live_size_is_within_the_raised_budget():
        """C-17: the repository's own live instructions file stays inside the budget.
    
        Derived, never pinned: the budget comes from `_budget_bytes()` (the script is
        its single owner) and the size is measured from the live file. A literal here
        would be a second owner of the number and would drift the moment the constant
        is retuned — which is precisely what Feature 082 does.
    
        The comparison is `size <= budget - 1` because the generator's `over` verdict
        fires at `size >= budget`, so a file of exactly `budget` bytes is already over.
        Stated as arithmetic, not re-derived: the verdict rule lives in the script.
        """
>       budget = _budget_bytes()
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def test_no_second_map_or_boundary_table_exists():
        roster = set(_roster_stages())
        assert roster, "the stage roster must parse before carrier uniqueness is judged"
        duplicates = []
        for path in _md_files():
            if path == OWNER:
                continue
            try:
                text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
                continue
            if "强制状态" not in text and "门禁处理" not in text:
                continue
            for header, rows in _tables(text.splitlines()):
                if "强制状态" in header and "强制门禁" in header:
                    for row in rows:
                        if len(row) >= 6 and row[0].strip("` ") in roster:
                            duplicates.append((str(path.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)), row[0]))
                            break
                if "门禁处理" in header and "边界" in header:
                    ids = {row[0].strip("` ") for row in rows if row}
                    if {"a", "b", "c", "d"} & ids:
                        duplicates.append((str(path.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)), "boundary table"))
                        break

_________________ test_no_second_document_claims_map_ownership _________________

    def test_no_second_document_claims_map_ownership():
        """A second claimant would be a document declaring itself the owner.
    
        ``唯一所有者`` as a phrase appears in plans that merely *point at* the owner
        ("put the map into the single owner"), so the detector requires an explicit
        ownership declaration line (``Owner`` + ``唯一所有者``) inside the opening
        block of a document that also names the map.
        """
        claims = []
        for path in _md_files():
            if path == OWNER:
                continue
            try:
                lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
            except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
                continue
            opening = "\n".join(lines[:30])
            if "同构映射" not in opening:
                continue
            for line in lines[:10]:
                if "Owner" in line and "唯一所有者" in line:
                    claims.append(str(path.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)))
                    break
>       assert not claims, (
            "only the classification owner may claim the map; other claimants: %r" % (claims,)
        )

________________________ test_ng_05_stdlib_only_engine _________________________

    def test_ng_05_stdlib_only_engine():
        tree = ast.parse(ENGINE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        imports = set()
        for node in ast.walk(tree):
            if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
                for alias in node.names:
                    imports.add(alias.name.split(".")[0])
            elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
                if node.level == 0 and node.module:
                    imports.add(node.module.split(".")[0])
        allowed = {
            "argparse", "contextlib", "errno", "fcntl", "hashlib", "json", "os", "re",
            "secrets", "shutil", "socket", "subprocess", "sys", "tempfile", "time",
            "datetime", "pathlib", "__future__",
        }
        external = imports - allowed
>       assert external == set(), f"non-stdlib imports: {sorted(external)}"
E       AssertionError: non-stdlib imports: ['script_interface_cli']
E       assert {'script_interface_cli'} == set()
E         
E         Extra items in the left set:
E         'script_interface_cli'
E         Use -v to get more diff

    def _execute_child(self, args, executable, preexec_fn, close_fds,
                       pass_fds, cwd, env,
                       startupinfo, creationflags, shell,
                       p2cread, p2cwrite,
                       c2pread, c2pwrite,
                       errread, errwrite,
                       restore_signals,
                       gid, gids, uid, umask,
                       start_new_session, process_group):
        """Execute program (POSIX version)"""
    
        if isinstance(args, (str, bytes)):
            args = [args]
        elif isinstance(args, os.PathLike):
            if shell:
                raise TypeError('path-like args is not allowed when '
                                'shell is true')
            args = [args]
        else:
            args = list(args)
    
        if shell:
            # On Android the default shell is at '/system/bin/sh'.
            unix_shell = ('/system/bin/sh' if
                      hasattr(sys, 'getandroidapilevel') else '/bin/sh')
            args = [unix_shell, "-c"] + args
            if executable:
                args[0] = executable
    
        if executable is None:
            executable = args[0]
    
        sys.audit("subprocess.Popen", executable, args, cwd, env)
    
        if (_USE_POSIX_SPAWN
                and os.path.dirname(executable)
                and preexec_fn is None
                and not close_fds
                and not pass_fds
                and cwd is None
                and (p2cread == -1 or p2cread > 2)
                and (c2pwrite == -1 or c2pwrite > 2)
                and (errwrite == -1 or errwrite > 2)
                and not start_new_session
                and process_group == -1
                and gid is None
                and gids is None
                and uid is None
                and umask < 0):
            self._posix_spawn(args, executable, env, restore_signals,
                              p2cread, p2cwrite,
                              c2pread, c2pwrite,
                              errread, errwrite)
            return
    
        orig_executable = executable
    
        # For transferring possible exec failure from child to parent.
        # Data format: "exception name:hex errno:description"
        # Pickle is not used; it is complex and involves memory allocation.
        errpipe_read, errpipe_write = os.pipe()
        # errpipe_write must not be in the standard io 0, 1, or 2 fd range.
        low_fds_to_close = []
        while errpipe_write < 3:
            low_fds_to_close.append(errpipe_write)
            errpipe_write = os.dup(errpipe_write)
        for low_fd in low_fds_to_close:
            os.close(low_fd)
        try:
            try:
                # We must avoid complex work that could involve
                # malloc or free in the child process to avoid
                # potential deadlocks, thus we do all this here.
                # and pass it to fork_exec()
    
                if env is not None:
                    env_list = []
                    for k, v in env.items():
                        k = os.fsencode(k)
                        if b'=' in k:
                            raise ValueError("illegal environment variable name")
                        env_list.append(k + b'=' + os.fsencode(v))
                else:
                    env_list = None  # Use execv instead of execve.
                executable = os.fsencode(executable)
                if os.path.dirname(executable):
                    executable_list = (executable,)
                else:
                    # This matches the behavior of os._execvpe().
                    executable_list = tuple(
                        os.path.join(os.fsencode(dir), executable)
                        for dir in os.get_exec_path(env))
                fds_to_keep = set(pass_fds)
                fds_to_keep.add(errpipe_write)
                self.pid = _fork_exec(
                        args, executable_list,
                        close_fds, tuple(sorted(map(int, fds_to_keep))),
                        cwd, env_list,
                        p2cread, p2cwrite, c2pread, c2pwrite,
                        errread, errwrite,
                        errpipe_read, errpipe_write,
                        restore_signals, start_new_session,
                        process_group, gid, gids, uid, umask,
                        preexec_fn, _USE_VFORK)
                self._child_created = True
            finally:
                # be sure the FD is closed no matter what
                os.close(errpipe_write)
    
            self._close_pipe_fds(p2cread, p2cwrite,
                                 c2pread, c2pwrite,
                                 errread, errwrite)
    
            # Wait for exec to fail or succeed; possibly raising an
            # exception (limited in size)
            errpipe_data = bytearray()
            while True:
                part = os.read(errpipe_read, 50000)
                errpipe_data += part
                if not part or len(errpipe_data) > 50000:
                    break
        finally:
            # be sure the FD is closed no matter what
            os.close(errpipe_read)
    
        if errpipe_data:
            try:
                pid, sts = os.waitpid(self.pid, 0)
                if pid == self.pid:
                    self._handle_exitstatus(sts)
                else:
                    self.returncode = sys.maxsize
            except ChildProcessError:
                pass
    
            try:
                exception_name, hex_errno, err_msg = (
                        errpipe_data.split(b':', 2))
                # The encoding here should match the encoding
                # written in by the subprocess implementations
                # like _posixsubprocess
                err_msg = err_msg.decode()
            except ValueError:
                exception_name = b'SubprocessError'
                hex_errno = b'0'
                err_msg = 'Bad exception data from child: {!r}'.format(
                              bytes(errpipe_data))
            child_exception_type = getattr(
                    builtins, exception_name.decode('ascii'),
                    SubprocessError)
            if issubclass(child_exception_type, OSError) and hex_errno:
                errno_num = int(hex_errno, 16)
                child_exec_never_called = (err_msg == "noexec")
                if child_exec_never_called:
                    err_msg = ""
                    # The error must be from chdir(cwd).
                    err_filename = cwd
                else:
                    err_filename = orig_executable
                if errno_num != 0:
                    err_msg = os.strerror(errno_num)
>               raise child_exception_type(errno_num, err_msg, err_filename)
E               PermissionError: [Errno 13] Permission denied: '/tmp/pytest-of-agent/pytest-689/c26-entry-point0/.venv/bin/specify'

    def test_skills_use_installed_absolute_shared_form():
        """Sibling skills reference shared docs via the installed-absolute shared path.
    
        Scans every markdown file in a skill, not just SKILL.md: the references/ files ship
        into `.specify/skills/<name>/` too, and a bare `shared/<type>/` path there is just as
        unresolvable in a client project. Scanning only the top-level file left three of them
        undetected while the test was red for an unrelated reason.
        """
        import re
    
        offenders = []
        scanned = 0
        bare = re.compile(r"(?<![\w./])shared/(" + "|".join(SHARED_TYPES) + r")/")
        for f in _iter_files("skills/**/*.md"):
            scanned += 1
            text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            if bare.search(text):
                offenders.append(str(f.relative_to(ROOT)))
        assert scanned > 100, (
            f"only {scanned} skill markdown files scanned — the glob looks truncated, which "
            f"would let an empty offender list pass without proving anything"
        )

    def test_c1_stdlib_only():
        tree = ast.parse(ENGINE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        mods = set()
        for node in ast.walk(tree):
            if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
                mods.update(a.name for a in node.names)
            elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module and node.level == 0:
                mods.add(node.module)
        stdlib = set(sys.stdlib_module_names) if hasattr(sys, "stdlib_module_names") else None
        if stdlib is None:  # Python 3.8/3.9
            import distutils.sysconfig  # noqa
            stdlib = None
        first_party_siblings = {"declaration_reader"}  # shipped beside the engine by the same mirror
        non_std = []
        for m in sorted(mods):
            top = m.split(".")[0]
            if stdlib is not None and top not in stdlib and m not in first_party_siblings:
                non_std.append(m)

_____ test_c11_no_dangling_guideline_pointer_on_either_instruction_surface _____

    def test_c11_no_dangling_guideline_pointer_on_either_instruction_surface():
        surfaces = (("template", TEMPLATE), ("live", LIVE))
        pointed: set[str] = set()
        for label, path in surfaces:
            names = set(GUIDELINE_POINTER_RE.findall(_text(path)))
            assert names, f"the {label} instruction surface carries no guideline pointer at all"
            pointed |= names
    
        # A pointer's whole job is to let a reader open the file, so the target that must exist
        # is the one the reader actually resolves: `.specify/shared/guidelines/<name>.md`.
        # Checking only the framework source leaves the downstream failure mode invisible —
        # proven by drill: with the runtime copy moved aside and the source intact, a
        # source-only version of this clause still passed. Both halves are asserted, because
        # the source is what regenerates the runtime copy.
        runtime_dir = ROOT / ".specify" / "shared" / "guidelines"
        dangling_runtime = sorted(n for n in pointed if not (runtime_dir / n).is_file())
        assert not dangling_runtime, (
            "instruction surfaces point at guidelines with no runtime copy under "
            ".specify/shared/guidelines/ — a reader following the pointer finds nothing, which "
            "is the delivery window FR-038 / SC-017 exists to close "
            f"(run scripts/python/sync-mirrors.py --write shared): {dangling_runtime}"
        )
        dangling_source = sorted(n for n in pointed if not (GUIDELINES / n).is_file())
        assert not dangling_source, (
            "instruction surfaces point at guidelines with no framework source under "
            f"shared/guidelines/ — the runtime copy cannot be regenerated: {dangling_source}"
        )
    
        # C-11(b): the remainder must not grow past the three structurally unpointed files.
        # Anti-vacuity: name the three and require them to exist, so an empty guidelines
        # directory cannot make the subset assertion pass on nothing.
        for name in UNPOINTED_BY_STRUCTURE:
            assert (GUIDELINES / name).is_file(), (
                f"C-11(b) names {name} as unpointed-by-structure but it is not in shared/guidelines/"
            )
        full = {p.name for p in GUIDELINES.glob("*.md")}
        remainder = full - pointed
        unexpected = sorted(remainder - set(UNPOINTED_BY_STRUCTURE))
>       assert not unexpected, (
            "a guideline lost its instruction-surface pointer, so it can no longer dangle "
            "loudly — coverage shrank silently. Either restore the pointer or amend "
            f"ambient-section.md C-11(b) with a recorded reason: {unexpected}"

    def run(*popenargs,
            input=None, capture_output=False, timeout=None, check=False, **kwargs):
        """Run command with arguments and return a CompletedProcess instance.
    
        The returned instance will have attributes args, returncode, stdout and
        stderr. By default, stdout and stderr are not captured, and those attributes
        will be None. Pass stdout=PIPE and/or stderr=PIPE in order to capture them.
    
        If check is True and the exit code was non-zero, it raises a
        CalledProcessError. The CalledProcessError object will have the return code
        in the returncode attribute, and output & stderr attributes if those streams
        were captured.
    
        If timeout is given, and the process takes too long, a TimeoutExpired
        exception will be raised.
    
        There is an optional argument "input", allowing you to
        pass bytes or a string to the subprocess's stdin.  If you use this argument
        you may not also use the Popen constructor's "stdin" argument, as
        it will be used internally.
    
        By default, all communication is in bytes, and therefore any "input" should
        be bytes, and the stdout and stderr will be bytes. If in text mode, any
        "input" should be a string, and stdout and stderr will be strings decoded
        according to locale encoding, or by "encoding" if set. Text mode is
        triggered by setting any of text, encoding, errors or universal_newlines.
    
        The other arguments are the same as for the Popen constructor.
        """
        if input is not None:
            if kwargs.get('stdin') is not None:
                raise ValueError('stdin and input arguments may not both be used.')
            kwargs['stdin'] = PIPE
    
        if capture_output:
            if kwargs.get('stdout') is not None or kwargs.get('stderr') is not None:
                raise ValueError('stdout and stderr arguments may not be used '
                                 'with capture_output.')
            kwargs['stdout'] = PIPE
            kwargs['stderr'] = PIPE
    
        with Popen(*popenargs, **kwargs) as process:
            try:
                stdout, stderr = process.communicate(input, timeout=timeout)
            except TimeoutExpired as exc:
                process.kill()
                if _mswindows:
                    # Windows accumulates the output in a single blocking
                    # read() call run on child threads, with the timeout
                    # being done in a join() on those threads.  communicate()
                    # _after_ kill() is required to collect that and add it
                    # to the exception.
                    exc.stdout, exc.stderr = process.communicate()
                else:
                    # POSIX _communicate already populated the output so
                    # far into the TimeoutExpired exception.
                    process.wait()
                raise
            except:  # Including KeyboardInterrupt, communicate handled that.
                process.kill()
                # We don't call process.wait() as .__exit__ does that for us.
                raise
            retcode = process.poll()
            if check and retcode:
>               raise CalledProcessError(retcode, process.args,
                                         output=stdout, stderr=stderr)
E               subprocess.CalledProcessError: Command '['/tmp/pytest-of-agent/pytest-689/test_default_install_creates_p0/.specify/scripts/bash/create-new-skill.sh', '--json', 'layout-int-b74920c1 - integration scenario']' returned non-zero exit status 1.

        were captured.
    
        If timeout is given, and the process takes too long, a TimeoutExpired
        exception will be raised.
    
        There is an optional argument "input", allowing you to
        pass bytes or a string to the subprocess's stdin.  If you use this argument
        you may not also use the Popen constructor's "stdin" argument, as
        it will be used internally.
    
        By default, all communication is in bytes, and therefore any "input" should
        be bytes, and the stdout and stderr will be bytes. If in text mode, any
        "input" should be a string, and stdout and stderr will be strings decoded
        according to locale encoding, or by "encoding" if set. Text mode is
        triggered by setting any of text, encoding, errors or universal_newlines.
    
        The other arguments are the same as for the Popen constructor.
        """
        if input is not None:
            if kwargs.get('stdin') is not None:
                raise ValueError('stdin and input arguments may not both be used.')
            kwargs['stdin'] = PIPE
    
        if capture_output:
            if kwargs.get('stdout') is not None or kwargs.get('stderr') is not None:
                raise ValueError('stdout and stderr arguments may not be used '
                                 'with capture_output.')
            kwargs['stdout'] = PIPE
            kwargs['stderr'] = PIPE

                stdout, stderr = process.communicate(input, timeout=timeout)
            except TimeoutExpired as exc:

                    # read() call run on child threads, with the timeout
                    # being done in a join() on those threads.  communicate()

                    # to the exception.

                    # far into the TimeoutExpired exception.
                    process.wait()
                raise
            except:  # Including KeyboardInterrupt, communicate handled that.
                process.kill()

                raise

    def run(*popenargs,
            input=None, capture_output=False, timeout=None, check=False, **kwargs):
        """Run command with arguments and return a CompletedProcess instance.
    
        The returned instance will have attributes args, returncode, stdout and
        stderr. By default, stdout and stderr are not captured, and those attributes
        will be None. Pass stdout=PIPE and/or stderr=PIPE in order to capture them.
    
        If check is True and the exit code was non-zero, it raises a
        CalledProcessError. The CalledProcessError object will have the return code
        in the returncode attribute, and output & stderr attributes if those streams
        were captured.
    
        If timeout is given, and the process takes too long, a TimeoutExpired
        exception will be raised.
    
        There is an optional argument "input", allowing you to
        pass bytes or a string to the subprocess's stdin.  If you use this argument
        you may not also use the Popen constructor's "stdin" argument, as
        it will be used internally.
    
        By default, all communication is in bytes, and therefore any "input" should
        be bytes, and the stdout and stderr will be bytes. If in text mode, any
        "input" should be a string, and stdout and stderr will be strings decoded
        according to locale encoding, or by "encoding" if set. Text mode is
        triggered by setting any of text, encoding, errors or universal_newlines.
    
        The other arguments are the same as for the Popen constructor.
        """
        if input is not None:
            if kwargs.get('stdin') is not None:
                raise ValueError('stdin and input arguments may not both be used.')
            kwargs['stdin'] = PIPE
    
        if capture_output:
            if kwargs.get('stdout') is not None or kwargs.get('stderr') is not None:
                raise ValueError('stdout and stderr arguments may not be used '
                                 'with capture_output.')
            kwargs['stdout'] = PIPE
            kwargs['stderr'] = PIPE
    
        with Popen(*popenargs, **kwargs) as process:
            try:
                stdout, stderr = process.communicate(input, timeout=timeout)
            except TimeoutExpired as exc:
                process.kill()
                if _mswindows:
                    # Windows accumulates the output in a single blocking
                    # read() call run on child threads, with the timeout
                    # being done in a join() on those threads.  communicate()
                    # _after_ kill() is required to collect that and add it
                    # to the exception.
                    exc.stdout, exc.stderr = process.communicate()
                else:
                    # POSIX _communicate already populated the output so
                    # far into the TimeoutExpired exception.
                    process.wait()
                raise
            except:  # Including KeyboardInterrupt, communicate handled that.
                process.kill()
                # We don't call process.wait() as .__exit__ does that for us.
                raise
            retcode = process.poll()
            if check and retcode:
